===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(String,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 121-161
Documentation:
/**
   * @param fnName The name of this function. This either the name of the
   *  variable to which the function is assigned or the name from the FUNCTION
   *  node.
   * @param fnNode The FUNCTION node of the function to inspect.
   * @return Whether the function node meets the minimum requirements for
   * inlining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   boolean doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(
122 |       final String fnName, Node fnNode) {
123 |     Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
124 | 
125 |     // Basic restrictions on functions that can be inlined:
126 |     // 0) The function is inlinable by convention
127 |     // 1) It contains a reference to itself.
128 |     // 2) It uses its parameters indirectly using "arguments" (it isn't
129 |     //    handled yet.
130 |     // 3) It references "eval". Inline a function containing eval can have
131 |     //    large performance implications.
132 | 
133 |     if (!compiler.getCodingConvention().isInlinableFunction(fnNode)) {
134 |       return false;
135 |     }
136 | 
137 |     final String fnRecursionName = fnNode.getFirstChild().getString();
138 |     Preconditions.checkState(fnRecursionName != null);
139 | 
140 |     // If the function references "arguments" directly in the function
141 |     boolean referencesArguments = NodeUtil.isNameReferenced(
142 |         block, "arguments", NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION);
143 | 
144 |     // or it references "eval" or one of its names anywhere.
145 |     Predicate<Node> p = new Predicate<Node>(){
146 |       @Override
147 |       public boolean apply(Node n) {
148 |         if (n.isName()) {
149 |           return n.getString().equals("eval")
150 |             || (!fnName.isEmpty()
151 |                 && n.getString().equals(fnName))
152 |             || (!fnRecursionName.isEmpty()
153 |                 && n.getString().equals(fnRecursionName));
154 |         }
155 |         return false;
156 |       }
157 |     };
158 | 
159 |     return !referencesArguments
160 |         && !NodeUtil.has(block, p, Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue());
161 |   }

===== M0030 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 519-537
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks if the given function matches the criteria for an inlinable
   * function, and if so, adds it to our set of inlinable functions.
   */
Method code:
519 |   boolean isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(Node fnNode) {
520 |     // Only inline single-statement functions
521 |     Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
522 | 
523 |     // Check if this function is suitable for direct replacement of a CALL node:
524 |     // a function that consists of single return that returns an expression.
525 |     if (!block.hasChildren()) {
526 |       // special case empty functions.
527 |       return true;
528 |     } else if (block.hasOneChild()) {
529 |       // Only inline functions that return something.
530 |       if (block.getFirstChild().isReturn()
531 |           && block.getFirstChild().getFirstChild() != null) {
532 |         return true;
533 |       }
534 |     }
535 | 
536 |     return false;
537 |   }

===== M0032 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

Original range: 414-444

/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
414 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
415 |       Preconditions.checkState(
416 |           n.isName()
417 |           || n.isString()
418 |           || n.isStringKey()
419 |           || n.isGetterDef()
420 |           || n.isSetterDef());
421 | 
422 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
423 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
424 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
425 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);
426 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
427 |           (parent.isGetProp() &&
428 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
429 |       if (n.isName() || isProperty) {
430 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
431 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
432 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
433 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
434 |           if (assertOnChange) {
435 |             String name = n.getString();
436 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
437 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
438 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
439 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
440 |           }
441 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
442 |         }
443 |       }
444 |     }

===== M0037 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.canInlineReferenceToFunction(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Set<String>,InliningMode,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 175-215
Documentation:
/**
   * @param t  The traversal use to reach the call site.
   * @param callNode The CALL node.
   * @param fnNode The function to evaluate for inlining.
   * @param needAliases A set of function parameter names that can not be
   *     used without aliasing. Returned by getUnsafeParameterNames().
   * @param mode Inlining mode to be used.
   * @param referencesThis Whether fnNode contains references to its this
   *     object.
   * @param containsFunctions Whether fnNode contains inner functions.
   * @return Whether the inlining can occur.
   */
Method code:
175 |   CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceToFunction(NodeTraversal t,
176 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode, Set<String> needAliases,
177 |       InliningMode mode, boolean referencesThis, boolean containsFunctions) {
178 |     // TODO(johnlenz): This function takes too many parameter, without
179 |     // context.  Modify the API to take a structure describing the function.
180 | 
181 |     // Allow direct function calls or "fn.call" style calls.
182 |     if (!isSupportedCallType(callNode)) {
183 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
184 |     }
185 | 
186 |     // Limit where functions that contain functions can be inline.  Introducing
187 |     // an inner function into another function can capture a variable and cause
188 |     // a memory leak.  This isn't a problem in the global scope as those values
189 |     // last until explicitly cleared.
190 |     if (containsFunctions) {
191 |       if (!assumeMinimumCapture && !t.inGlobalScope()) {
192 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Allow inlining into any scope without local names or
193 |         // inner functions.
194 |         return CanInlineResult.NO;
195 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isWithinLoop(callNode)) {
196 |         // An inner closure maybe relying on a local value holding a value for a
197 |         // single iteration through a loop.
198 |         return CanInlineResult.NO;
199 |       }
200 |     }
201 | 
202 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Add support for 'apply'
203 |     if (referencesThis && !NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
204 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Allow 'this' references to be replaced with a
205 |       // global 'this' object.
206 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
207 |     }
208 | 
209 |     if (mode == InliningMode.DIRECT) {
210 |       return canInlineReferenceDirectly(callNode, fnNode);
211 |     } else {
212 |       return canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(
213 |           t, callNode, fnNode, needAliases);
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1105-1125
Documentation:

Method code:
1105 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1106 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1107 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1108 |       return s;
1109 |     }
1110 | 
1111 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1112 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1113 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1114 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1115 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1116 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1117 |       // crazy.
1118 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1119 |         sb.append(c);
1120 |       } else {
1121 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1122 |       }
1123 |     }
1124 |     return sb.toString();
1125 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isConstantName(Node)

Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.
   *
   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:
   * <ol>
   * <li>In CodingConventionAnnotator, any name that matches the
   *     {@link CodingConvention#isConstant(String)} is annotated with an
   *     IS_CONSTANT_NAME property.
   * <li>The normalize pass renames any variable with the IS_CONSTANT_NAME
   *     annotation and that is initialized to a constant value with
   *     a variable name inlucding $$constant.
   * <li>Return true here if the variable includes $$constant in its name.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param node A NAME or STRING node
   * @return True if the variable is constant
   */
Method code:
2793 |   static boolean isConstantName(Node node) {
2794 |     return node.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
2795 |   }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 156-260
Documentation:

Method code:
156 |     @Override
157 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
158 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
159 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
160 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
161 |       if (inExterns && node.isName() && parent.isParamList()) {
162 |         return;
163 |       }
164 | 
165 |       Definition def =
166 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
167 |       if (def != null) {
168 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
169 |         if (name != null) {
170 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
171 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
172 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
173 |               !rValue.isFunction()) {
174 | 
175 |             // Unhandled complex expression
176 |             Definition unknownDef =
177 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
178 |             def = unknownDef;
179 |           }
180 | 
181 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
182 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
183 |           if (inExterns) {
184 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
185 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
186 | 
187 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
188 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
189 | 
190 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
191 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
192 |             // literal key.
193 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
194 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
195 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
196 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
197 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
198 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
199 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
200 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
201 |                   }
202 |                 }
203 |               }
204 | 
205 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
206 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
207 |               }
208 |             }
209 |           }
210 | 
211 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
212 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
213 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
214 |                                                    def,
215 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
216 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
217 |                                                    inExterns));
218 |         }
219 |       }
220 | 
221 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && parent.isExprResult()) {
222 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
223 |         if (name != null) {
224 | 
225 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
226 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
227 | 
228 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
229 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
230 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
231 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
232 | 
233 |           boolean dropStub = false;
234 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
235 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
236 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
237 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
238 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
239 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
240 |                   dropStub = true;
241 |                   break;
242 |                 }
243 |               }
244 |             }
245 |           }
246 | 
247 |           if (!dropStub) {
248 |             // Incomplete definition
249 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
250 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
251 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
252 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
253 |                                                      definition,
254 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
255 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
256 |                                                      inExterns));
257 |           }
258 |         }
259 |       }
260 |     }

===== M0057 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

===== M0088 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0102 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidateFunctions.findFunctionExpressions(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 212-235
Documentation:
/**
     * Find function expressions that are called directly in the form of
     *   (function(a,b,...){...})(a,b,...)
     * or
     *   (function(a,b,...){...}).call(this,a,b, ...)
     */
Method code:
212 |     public void findFunctionExpressions(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
213 |       switch (n.getType()) {
214 |         // Functions expressions in the form of:
215 |         //   (function(){})();
216 |         case Token.CALL:
217 |           Node fnNode = null;
218 |           if (n.getFirstChild().isFunction()) {
219 |             fnNode = n.getFirstChild();
220 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(n)) {
221 |             Node fnIdentifingNode = n.getFirstChild().getFirstChild();
222 |             if (fnIdentifingNode.isFunction()) {
223 |               fnNode = fnIdentifingNode;
224 |             }
225 |           }
226 | 
227 |           // If a interesting function was discovered, add it.
228 |           if (fnNode != null) {
229 |             Function fn = new FunctionExpression(fnNode, callsSeen++);
230 |             maybeAddFunction(fn, t.getModule());
231 |             anonFns.put(fnNode, fn.getName());
232 |           }
233 |           break;
234 |       }
235 |     }

===== M0103 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 560-607
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
560 |     private void extractForInitializer(
561 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
562 | 
563 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
564 |         next = c.getNext();
565 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
566 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
567 |         switch (c.getType()) {
568 |           case Token.LABEL:
569 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
570 |             break;
571 |           case Token.FOR:
572 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
573 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
574 |               if (first.isVar()) {
575 |                 // Transform:
576 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
577 |                 // to:
578 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
579 |                 Node newStatement = first;
580 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
581 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
582 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
583 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
584 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
585 |               }
586 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
587 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
588 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
589 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
590 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
591 | 
592 |               Node newStatement;
593 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
594 |               // but are handled differently.
595 |               if (init.isVar()) {
596 |                 newStatement = init;
597 |               } else {
598 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
599 |               }
600 | 
601 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
602 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
603 |             }
604 |             break;
605 |         }
606 |       }
607 |     }

83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {

85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());

87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),

246 |   private void initializeBuiltInTypes() {

270 | 
271 |     // Top Level Prototype (the One)

===== M0124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1016-1103
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1016 |   private static String strEscape(
1017 |       String s, char quote,
1018 |       String doublequoteEscape,
1019 |       String singlequoteEscape,
1020 |       String backslashEscape,
1021 |       CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder,
1022 |       boolean useSlashV) {
1023 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1024 |     sb.append(quote);
1025 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1026 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1027 |       switch (c) {
1028 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1029 |         case '\u000B':
1030 |           if (useSlashV) {
1031 |             sb.append("\\v");
1032 |           } else {
1033 |             sb.append("\\x0B");
1034 |           }
1035 |           break;
1036 |         // From the SingleEscapeCharacter grammar production.
1037 |         case '\b': sb.append("\\b"); break;
1038 |         case '\f': sb.append("\\f"); break;
1039 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1040 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1041 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1042 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1043 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1044 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1045 | 
1046 |         // From LineTerminators (ES5 Section 7.3, Table 3)
1047 |         case '\u2028': sb.append("\\u2028"); break;
1048 |         case '\u2029': sb.append("\\u2029"); break;
1049 | 
1050 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1051 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1052 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1053 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1054 |             sb.append("\\>");
1055 |           } else {
1056 |             sb.append(c);
1057 |           }
1058 |           break;
1059 |         case '<':
1060 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1061 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1062 | 
1063 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1064 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1065 | 
1066 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1067 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1068 |             sb.append("<\\");
1069 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1070 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1071 |             sb.append("<\\");
1072 |           } else {
1073 |             sb.append(c);
1074 |           }
1075 |           break;
1076 |         default:
1077 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1078 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1079 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1080 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1081 |               sb.append(c);
1082 |             } else {
1083 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1084 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1085 |             }
1086 |           } else {
1087 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1088 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1089 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1090 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1091 |               sb.append(c);
1092 |             } else {
1093 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1094 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1095 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1096 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1097 |             }
1098 |           }
1099 |       }
1100 |     }
1101 |     sb.append(quote);
1102 |     return sb.toString();
1103 |   }

===== M0125 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 164-180
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0151 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1508-1557
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1508 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1509 |     if (type != node.getType()
1510 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1511 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1512 |       return false;
1513 |     }
1514 | 
1515 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1516 |       return false;
1517 |     }
1518 | 
1519 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1520 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1521 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1522 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1523 |         return false;
1524 |       }
1525 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1526 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1527 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1528 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1529 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1530 |           return false;
1531 |         }
1532 |       }
1533 | 
1534 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1535 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1536 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1537 |         return false;
1538 |       }
1539 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1540 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1541 |         return false;
1542 |       }
1543 |     }
1544 | 
1545 |     if (recurse) {
1546 |       Node n, n2;
1547 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1548 |            n != null;
1549 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1550 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1551 |           return false;
1552 |         }
1553 |       }
1554 |     }
1555 | 
1556 |     return true;
1557 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.trimCanidatesUsingOnCost()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 665-682
Documentation:
/**
   * Remove entries from the list of candidates that can't be inlined.
   */
Method code:
665 |   void trimCanidatesUsingOnCost() {
666 |     Iterator<Entry<String, FunctionState>> i;
667 |     for (i = fns.entrySet().iterator(); i.hasNext();) {
668 |       FunctionState fs = i.next().getValue();
669 |       if (fs.hasReferences()) {
670 |         // Only inline function if it decreases the code size.
671 |         boolean lowersCost = mimimizeCost(fs);
672 |         if (!lowersCost) {
673 |           // It shouldn't be inlined; remove it from the list.
674 |           i.remove();
675 |         }
676 |       } else if (!fs.canRemove()) {
677 |         // Don't bother tracking functions without references that can't be
678 |         // removed.
679 |         i.remove();
680 |       }
681 |     }
682 |   }

===== M0167 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.findModifiedParameters(Node,Node,Set<String>,Set<String>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 195-219
Documentation:
/**
   * Check for uses of the named value that imply a pass-by-value
   * parameter is expected.  This is used to prevent cases like:
   *
   *   function (x) {
   *     x=2;
   *     return x;
   *   }
   *
   * We don't want "undefined" to be substituted for "x", and get
   *   undefined=2
   *
   * @param n The node in question.
   * @param parent The parent of the node.
   * @param names The set of names to check.
   * @param unsafe The set of names that require aliases.
   * @param inInnerFunction Whether the inspection is occurring on a inner
   *     function.
   */
Method code:
195 |   private static Set<String> findModifiedParameters(
196 |       Node n, Node parent, Set<String> names, Set<String> unsafe,
197 |       boolean inInnerFunction) {
198 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(unsafe != null);
199 |     if (n.isName()) {
200 |       if (names.contains(n.getString())) {
201 |         if (inInnerFunction || canNameValueChange(n, parent)) {
202 |           unsafe.add(n.getString());
203 |         }
204 |       }
205 |     } else if (n.isFunction()) {
206 |       // A function parameter can not be replaced with a direct inlined value
207 |       // if it is referred to by an inner function. The inner function
208 |       // can out live the call we are replacing, so inner function must
209 |       // capture a unique name.  This approach does not work within loop
210 |       // bodies so those are forbidden elsewhere.
211 |       inInnerFunction = true;
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     for (Node c : n.children()) {
215 |       findModifiedParameters(c, n, names, unsafe, inInnerFunction);
216 |     }
217 | 
218 |     return unsafe;
219 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)

/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

155 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inliningLowersCost(JSModule,Node,Collection<? extends Reference>,Set<String>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 736-792
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine if inlining the function is likely to reduce the code size.
   * @param namesToAlias
   */
Method code:
736 |   boolean inliningLowersCost(
737 |       JSModule fnModule, Node fnNode, Collection<? extends Reference> refs,
738 |       Set<String> namesToAlias, boolean isRemovable, boolean referencesThis) {
739 |     int referenceCount = refs.size();
740 |     if (referenceCount == 0) {
741 |       return true;
742 |     }
743 | 
744 |     int referencesUsingBlockInlining = 0;
745 | 
746 |     boolean checkModules = isRemovable && fnModule != null;
747 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
748 | 
749 |     for (Reference ref : refs) {
750 |       if (ref.mode == InliningMode.BLOCK) {
751 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining++;
752 |       }
753 | 
754 |       // Check if any of the references cross the module boundaries.
755 |       if (checkModules && ref.module != null) {
756 |         if (ref.module != fnModule &&
757 |             !moduleGraph.dependsOn(ref.module, fnModule)) {
758 |           // Calculate the cost as if the function were non-removable,
759 |           // if it still lowers the cost inline it.
760 |           isRemovable = false;
761 |           checkModules = false;  // no need to check additional modules.
762 |         }
763 |       }
764 |     }
765 | 
766 |     int referencesUsingDirectInlining = referenceCount -
767 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining;
768 | 
769 |     // Don't bother calculating the cost of function for simple functions where
770 |     // possible.
771 |     // However, when inlining a complex function, even a single reference may be
772 |     // larger than the original function if there are many returns (resulting
773 |     // in additional assignments) or many parameters that need to be aliased
774 |     // so use the cost estimating.
775 |     if (referenceCount == 1 && isRemovable &&
776 |         referencesUsingDirectInlining == 1) {
777 |       return true;
778 |     }
779 | 
780 |     int callCost = estimateCallCost(fnNode, referencesThis);
781 |     int overallCallCost = callCost * referenceCount;
782 | 
783 |     int costDeltaDirect = inlineCostDelta(
784 |         fnNode, namesToAlias, InliningMode.DIRECT);
785 |     int costDeltaBlock = inlineCostDelta(
786 |         fnNode, namesToAlias, InliningMode.BLOCK);
787 | 
788 |     return doesLowerCost(fnNode, overallCallCost,
789 |         referencesUsingDirectInlining, costDeltaDirect,
790 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining, costDeltaBlock,
791 |         isRemovable);
792 |   }

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isObjectCallMethod(Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1936-1948
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether node is a call to methodName.
   *    a.f(...)
   *    a['f'](...)
   */
Method code:
1936 |   static boolean isObjectCallMethod(Node callNode, String methodName) {
1937 |     if (callNode.isCall()) {
1938 |       Node functionIndentifyingExpression = callNode.getFirstChild();
1939 |       if (isGet(functionIndentifyingExpression)) {
1940 |         Node last = functionIndentifyingExpression.getLastChild();
1941 |         if (last != null && last.isString()) {
1942 |           String propName = last.getString();
1943 |           return (propName.equals(methodName));
1944 |         }
1945 |       }
1946 |     }
1947 |     return false;
1948 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,boolean,boolean,int,SourceMap,SourceMap.DetailLevel,Charset,boolean)

Original range: 684-720
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
684 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
685 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  boolean preferEndOfFileBreak,
686 |                                  int lineLengthThreshold,
687 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
688 |                                  SourceMap.DetailLevel sourceMapDetailLevel,
689 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
690 |                                  boolean tagAsStrict) {
691 |     Preconditions.checkState(sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
692 | 
693 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
694 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
695 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
696 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
697 |             lineBreak, preferEndOfFileBreak, lineLengthThreshold,
698 |             createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel)
699 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
700 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);
701 |     CodeGenerator cg =
702 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
703 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
704 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
705 | 
706 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
707 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
708 |     }
709 | 
710 |     cg.add(root);
711 |     mcp.endFile();
712 | 
713 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
714 | 
715 |     if (createSourceMap) {
716 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     return code;
720 |   }

176 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
177 |       if (!node.isCall() && !node.isNew()) {
178 |         return;
179 |       }

76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.
82 |       Preconditions.checkState(
83 |           !declarationRoot.isFunction() ||
84 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));

774 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {

===== M0249 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 210-238
Documentation:

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }

===== M0278 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 463-483
Documentation:

Method code:
463 |     @Override
464 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
465 |       if (lineBreak) {
466 |         if (sawFunction) {
467 |           startNewLine();
468 |           sawFunction = false;
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
473 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
474 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
475 |       int len = code.length();
476 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
477 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
478 |         if (ch == ';') {
479 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
480 |         }
481 |       }
482 |       maybeCutLine();
483 |     }

===== M0281 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

   * Determines if the type of the value of the rhs expression can
   * be a function node.

Method code:
77 |   private static boolean definitionTypeContainsFunctionType(Definition def) {
78 |     Node rhs = def.getRValue();
79 |     if (rhs == null) {
80 |       return true;
81 |     }
82 | 
83 |     switch (rhs.getType()) {
84 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
85 |       case Token.AND:
86 |       case Token.CALL:
87 |       case Token.GETPROP:
88 |       case Token.GETELEM:
89 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
90 |       case Token.HOOK:
91 |       case Token.NAME:
92 |       case Token.NEW:

95 |       default:
96 |         return false;

===== M0293 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 948-1007
Documentation:

Method code:
948 |   private void toString(
949 |       StringBuilder sb,
950 |       boolean printSource,
951 |       boolean printAnnotations,
952 |       boolean printType) {
953 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
954 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
955 |       sb.append(' ');
956 |       sb.append(getString());
957 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
958 |       sb.append(' ');
959 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
960 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
961 |       // toStringTree.
962 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
963 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
964 |       } else {
965 |         sb.append(first.getString());
966 |       }
967 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
968 |       sb.append(' ');
969 |       sb.append(getDouble());
970 |     }
971 |     if (printSource) {
972 |       int lineno = getLineno();
973 |       if (lineno != -1) {
974 |         sb.append(' ');
975 |         sb.append(lineno);
976 |       }
977 |     }
978 | 
979 |     if (printAnnotations) {
980 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
981 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
982 |         int type = keys[i];
983 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
984 |         sb.append(" [");
985 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
986 |         sb.append(": ");
987 |         String value;
988 |         switch (type) {
989 |           default:
990 |             value = x.toString();
991 |             break;
992 |         }
993 |         sb.append(value);
994 |         sb.append(']');
995 |       }
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     if (printType) {
999 |       if (jsType != null) {
1000 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1001 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1002 |           sb.append(" : ");
1003 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1004 |         }
1005 |       }
1006 |     }
1007 |   }

===== M0299 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidatesReferences.maybeAddReference(NodeTraversal,FunctionState,Node,JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 490-515
Documentation:

Method code:
490 |     void maybeAddReference(NodeTraversal t, FunctionState fs,
491 |         Node callNode, JSModule module) {
492 |       if (!fs.canInline()) {
493 |         return;
494 |       }
495 | 
496 |       boolean referenceAdded = false;
497 |       InliningMode mode = fs.canInlineDirectly()
498 |            ? InliningMode.DIRECT : InliningMode.BLOCK;
499 |       referenceAdded = maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(
500 |           t, fs, callNode, module, mode);
501 |       if (!referenceAdded &&
502 |           mode == InliningMode.DIRECT && blockFunctionInliningEnabled) {
503 |         // This reference can not be directly inlined, see if
504 |         // block replacement inlining is possible.
505 |         mode = InliningMode.BLOCK;
506 |         referenceAdded = maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(
507 |             t, fs, callNode, module, mode);
508 |       }
509 | 
510 |       if (!referenceAdded) {
511 |         // Don't try to remove a function if we can't inline all
512 |         // the references.
513 |         fs.setRemove(false);
514 |       }
515 |     }

===== M0302 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 135-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
135 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
136 |     if (node.isName()) {
137 |       String name = node.getString();
138 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
139 |         return name;
140 |       } else {
141 |         return null;
142 |       }
143 |     } else if (node.isGetProp()) {
144 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
145 |     }
146 |     return null;
147 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformBlock(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 221-236
Documentation:

Method code:
221 |   private Node transformBlock(AstNode node) {
222 |     Node irNode = transform(node);
223 |     if (!irNode.isBlock()) {
224 |       if (irNode.isEmpty()) {
225 |         irNode.setType(Token.BLOCK);
226 |         irNode.setWasEmptyNode(true);
227 |       } else {
228 |         Node newBlock = newNode(Token.BLOCK, irNode);
229 |         newBlock.setLineno(irNode.getLineno());
230 |         newBlock.setCharno(irNode.getCharno());
231 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newBlock, node);
232 |         irNode = newBlock;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 |     return irNode;
236 |   }

===== M0310 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 640-672
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
640 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
641 |       Preconditions.checkState(
642 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
643 |       Node previous = null;
644 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
645 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
646 |       // are already in the right place.
647 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
648 |         previous = current;
649 |         current = current.getNext();
650 |       }
651 | 
652 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
653 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
654 |       while (current != null) {
655 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
656 |         Node next = current.getNext();
657 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
658 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
659 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
660 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
661 | 
662 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
663 |           // previous declarations).
664 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
665 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
666 |         } else {
667 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
668 |           previous = current;
669 |         }
670 |         current = next;
671 |       }
672 |     }

/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1444 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1445 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1446 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1450 |     Node n, n2;
1451 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1452 |          res == null && n != null;
1453 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1454 |       if (node2 == null) {
1455 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1456 |       }
1457 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);

1460 |       }
1461 |     }

===== M0312 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0317 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

===== M0334 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0345 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processStringLiteral(StringLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 910-937
Documentation:

Method code:
910 |     @Override
911 |     Node processStringLiteral(StringLiteral literalNode) {
912 |       String value = literalNode.getValue();
913 |       Node n = newStringNode(value);
914 |       if (value.indexOf('\u000B') != -1) {
915 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In JavaScript, there are 3 ways to
916 |         // represent a vertical tab: \v, \x0B, \u000B.
917 |         // The \v notation was added later, and is not understood
918 |         // on IE. So we need to preserve it as-is. This is really
919 |         // obnoxious, because we do not have a good way to represent
920 |         // how the original string was encoded without making the
921 |         // representation of strings much more complicated.
922 |         //
923 |         // To handle this, we look at the original source test, and
924 |         // mark the string as \v-encoded or not. If a string is
925 |         // \v encoded, then all the vertical tabs in that string
926 |         // will be encoded with a \v.
927 |         int start = literalNode.getAbsolutePosition();
928 |         int end = start + literalNode.getLength();
929 |         if (start < sourceString.length() &&
930 |             (sourceString.substring(
931 |                  start, Math.min(sourceString.length(), end))
932 |              .indexOf("\\v") != -1)) {
933 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.SLASH_V, true);
934 |         }
935 |       }
936 |       return n;

===== M0356 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

===== M0365 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }
