===== M0384 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-732
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
128 | 
129 |     switch (type) {
130 |       case Token.TRY: {
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");
136 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 | 
138 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
139 |         // isn't a catch block
140 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
141 |         if (catchblock != null) {
142 |           add(catchblock);
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         if (childCount == 3) {
146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }
151 | 
152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {
182 |           add("var ");
183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
189 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
198 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {
202 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
210 |         add("[");
211 |         addArrayList(first);
212 |         add("]");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
216 |         add("(");
217 |         addList(first);
218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.COMMA:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
223 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.NEG: {
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
245 | 
246 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
250 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
251 |         } else {
252 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }
255 | 
256 |         break;
257 |       }
258 | 
259 |       case Token.HOOK: {
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
263 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
264 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
265 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
266 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
267 |         break;
268 |       }
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REGEXP:
271 |         if (!first.isString() ||
272 |             !last.isString()) {
273 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
277 | 
278 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
279 |         if (childCount == 2) {
280 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
281 |         } else {
282 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
283 |           add(regexp);
284 |         }
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
288 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
289 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
290 |         }
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
292 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add("(");
295 |         }
296 | 
297 |         add("function");
298 |         add(first);
299 | 
300 |         add(first.getNext());
301 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
302 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
303 | 
304 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
305 |           add(")");
306 |         }
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
314 | 
315 |         // Get methods are unnamed
316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
317 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
324 |           add("set ");
325 |         }
326 | 
327 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
328 |         String name = n.getString();
329 |         Node fn = first;
330 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
331 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
332 | 
333 |         // Add the property name.
334 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
335 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
336 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
337 |             // unicode escaped.
338 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
339 |           add(name);
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
342 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
343 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
344 |             cc.addNumber(d);
345 |           } else {
346 |             addJsString(n);
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 | 
350 |         add(parameters);
351 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
355 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
356 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }
359 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
360 |         if (preserveBlock) {
361 |           cc.beginBlock();
362 |         }
363 | 
364 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
366 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
367 |                 !preserveBlock &&
368 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
370 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
372 | 
373 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
374 |           if (c.isVar()) {
375 |             cc.endStatement();
376 |           }
377 | 
378 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
379 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
380 |           }
381 | 
382 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
383 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
385 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
386 |           }
387 |         }
388 |         if (preserveBlock) {
389 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
390 |         }
391 |         break;
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext());
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
406 |           add(")");
407 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),
450 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {
453 |           add("(");
454 |         }
455 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }
459 |         add(".");
460 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {
491 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         cc.addConstant("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         cc.addConstant("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         cc.addConstant("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
632 |         Preconditions.checkState(
633 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
634 |         addJsString(n);
635 |         break;
636 | 
637 |       case Token.STRING:
638 |         Preconditions.checkState(
639 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
640 |         addJsString(n);
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.DELPROP:
644 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
645 |         add("delete ");
646 |         add(first);
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
650 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
651 |         if (needsParens) {
652 |           add("(");
653 |         }
654 |         add("{");
655 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
656 |           if (c != first) {
657 |             cc.listSeparator();
658 |           }
659 | 
660 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
661 |             add(c);
662 |           } else {
663 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
664 |             String key = c.getString();
665 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
666 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
667 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
668 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
669 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
670 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
671 |                 // were unicode escaped.
672 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
673 |               add(key);
674 |             } else {
675 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
676 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
677 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
678 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
679 |               } else {
680 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
681 |               }
682 |             }
683 |             add(":");
684 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
685 |           }
686 |         }
687 |         add("}");
688 |         if (needsParens) {
689 |           add(")");
690 |         }
691 |         break;
692 |       }
693 | 
694 |       case Token.SWITCH:
695 |         add("switch(");
696 |         add(first);
697 |         add(")");
698 |         cc.beginBlock();
699 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
700 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
701 |         break;
702 | 
703 |       case Token.CASE:
704 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
705 |         add("case ");
706 |         add(first);
707 |         addCaseBody(last);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
711 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
712 |         add("default");
713 |         addCaseBody(first);
714 |         break;
715 | 
716 |       case Token.LABEL:
717 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
718 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
719 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
720 |         }
721 |         add(first);
722 |         add(":");
723 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
724 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
725 |         break;
726 | 
727 |       default:
728 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
732 |   }

===== M0386 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 967-994
Documentation:

Method code:
967 |   private String jsString(String s, boolean useSlashV) {
968 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
969 | 
970 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
971 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
972 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
973 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
974 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
975 |       }
976 |     }
977 | 
978 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
979 |     char quote;
980 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
981 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
982 |       quote = '\'';
983 |       doublequote = "\"";
984 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
985 |     } else {
986 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
987 |       quote = '\"';
988 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
989 |       singlequote = "\'";
990 |     }
991 | 
992 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
993 |         outputCharsetEncoder, useSlashV);
994 |   }

===== M0390 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFunctionType(JSType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1279-1285
Documentation:
/**
   * @param parameters the function's parameters or {@code null}
   *        to indicate that the parameter types are unknown.
   * @param returnType the function's return type or {@code null} to indicate
   *        that the return type is unknown.
   */
Method code:
1279 |   public FunctionType createFunctionType(
1280 |       JSType returnType, Node parameters) {

===== M0410 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0413 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 37-65
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
37 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
38 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
39 |     // can be simplified.
40 | 
41 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
42 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
43 |     if (parent == null) {
44 |       return null;
45 |     }
46 | 
47 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
48 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
49 |     } else if (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
50 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
51 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
52 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
53 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
54 |       }
55 |     } else if (parent.isAssign() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
56 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
57 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
58 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
59 |           isExtern);
60 |     } else if (parent.isParamList()) {
61 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
62 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
63 |     }
64 |     return null;
65 |   }

===== M0416 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getCount(Node,Predicate<Node>,Predicate<Node>)

Original range: 2683-2698
Documentation:
/**
   * @return The number of times the the predicate is true for the node
   * or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2683 |   static int getCount(
2684 |       Node n, Predicate<Node> pred, Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2685 |     int total = 0;
2686 | 
2687 |     if (pred.apply(n)) {
2688 |       total++;
2689 |     }
2690 | 
2691 |     if (traverseChildrenPred.apply(n)) {
2692 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2693 |         total += getCount(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred);
2694 |       }
2695 |     }
2696 | 
2697 |     return total;
2698 |   }

87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {

===== M0423 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.estimateCallCost(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 832-850
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets an estimate of the cost in characters of making the function call:
   * the sum of the identifiers and the separators.
   * @param referencesThis
   */
Method code:
832 |   private static int estimateCallCost(Node fnNode, boolean referencesThis) {
833 |     Node argsNode = NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode);
834 |     int numArgs = argsNode.getChildCount();
835 | 
836 |     int callCost = NAME_COST_ESTIMATE + PAREN_COST;
837 |     if (numArgs > 0) {
838 |       callCost += (numArgs * NAME_COST_ESTIMATE) + ((numArgs - 1) * COMMA_COST);
839 |     }
840 | 
841 |     if (referencesThis) {
842 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Update this if we start supporting inlining
843 |       // other functions that reference this.
844 |       // The only functions that reference this that are currently inlined
845 |       // are those that are called via ".call" with an explicit "this".
846 |       callCost += 5 + 5;  // ".call" + "this,"
847 |     }
848 | 
849 |     return callCost;
850 |   }

===== M0425 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1364-1401
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1364 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1365 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1366 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1367 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1368 |           options.closurePass) {
1369 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1370 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1371 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1372 |           continue;
1373 |         }
1374 |       }
1375 | 
1376 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1377 | 
1378 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1379 |       if (n == null) {
1380 |         continue;
1381 |       }
1382 | 
1383 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1384 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1385 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1386 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1387 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1388 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1389 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1390 | 
1391 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1392 | 
1393 |         externs.add(input);
1394 |         staleInputs = true;
1395 |       }
1396 |     }
1397 | 
1398 |     if (staleInputs) {
1399 |       repartitionInputs();
1400 |     }
1401 |   }

===== M0430 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 861-881
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the Node is a DO or FUNCTION (with or without
   * labels).
   */
Method code:
861 |   private boolean isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node n) {
862 |     if (n.isLabel()) {
863 |       Node labeledStatement = n.getLastChild();
864 |       if (!labeledStatement.isBlock()) {
865 |         return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(labeledStatement);
866 |       } else {
867 |         // For labels with block children, we need to ensure that a
868 |         // labeled FUNCTION or DO isn't generated when extraneous BLOCKs
869 |         // are skipped.
870 |         if (getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2) == 1) {
871 |           return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(getFirstNonEmptyChild(n));
872 |         } else {
873 |           // Either a empty statement or an block with more than one child,
874 |           // way it isn't a FUNCTION or DO.
875 |           return false;
876 |         }
877 |       }
878 |     } else {
879 |       return (n.isFunction() || n.isDo());
880 |     }
881 |   }

     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line

Method code:
489 |     @Override
490 |     void maybeCutLine() {
491 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
492 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
493 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
494 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
495 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
496 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
497 |           prevCutPosition = position;
498 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
499 |           lineIndex++;
500 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
501 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
502 |         } else {
503 |           startNewLine();
504 |         }
505 |       }
506 |     }

===== M0436 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidatesReferences.checkNameUsage(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 549-588
Documentation:
/**
     * Find functions that can be inlined.
     */
Method code:
549 |     private void checkNameUsage(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
550 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isName());
551 | 
552 |       if (isCandidateUsage(n)) {
553 |         return;
554 |       }
555 | 
556 |       // Other refs to a function name remove its candidacy for inlining
557 |       String name = n.getString();
558 |       FunctionState fs = fns.get(name);
559 |       if (fs == null) {
560 |         return;
561 |       }
562 | 
563 |       // Unlike normal call/new parameters, references passed to
564 |       // JSCompiler_ObjectPropertyString are not aliases of a value, but
565 |       // a reference to the name itself, as such the value of the name is
566 |       // unknown and can not be inlined.
567 |       if (parent.isNew()) {
568 |         Node target = parent.getFirstChild();
569 |         if (target.isName() && target.getString().equals(
570 |             ObjectPropertyStringPreprocess.EXTERN_OBJECT_PROPERTY_STRING)) {
571 |           // This method is going to be replaced so don't inline it anywhere.
572 |           fs.setInline(false);
573 |         }
574 |       }
575 | 
576 |       // If the name is being assigned to it can not be inlined.
577 |       if (parent.isAssign() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
578 |         // e.g. bar = something; <== we can't inline "bar"
579 |         // so mark the function as uninlinable.
580 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Should we just remove it from fns here?
581 |         fs.setInline(false);
582 |       } else {
583 |         // e.g. var fn = bar; <== we can't inline "bar"
584 |         // As this reference can't be inlined mark the function as
585 |         // unremovable.
586 |         fs.setRemove(false);
587 |       }
588 |     }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 120-159
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   @Override
121 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
122 |     new NodeTraversal(
123 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
124 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
125 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
126 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
127 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
128 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
129 |     }
130 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
131 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
132 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
133 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
134 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
135 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
136 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
137 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
138 |     //   function f() {
139 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
140 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
141 |     //   }
142 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
143 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
144 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
145 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
146 |         .process(externs, root);
147 | 
148 |     FindExposeAnnotations findExposeAnnotations = new FindExposeAnnotations();
149 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
150 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
151 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
152 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
153 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
157 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
158 |     }
159 |   }

/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
223 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
224 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
225 | 
226 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
227 |       // which are var_args.
228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
229 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();

234 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
235 |

237 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
238 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
239 |         }
240 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
241 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
242 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
243 |         }
244 |       }

===== M0451 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 380-409
Documentation:

Method code:
380 |     @Override
381 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
382 |       switch (n.getType()) {
383 |         case Token.WHILE:
384 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
385 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
386 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
387 |             Node empty = IR.empty();
388 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
389 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
390 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
391 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
392 |           }
393 |           break;
394 | 
395 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
396 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
397 |           break;
398 | 
399 |         case Token.NAME:
400 |         case Token.STRING:
401 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
402 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
403 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
404 |           if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalizedObfuscated()) {
405 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
406 |           }
407 |           break;
408 |       }
409 |     }

2814 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2815 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());

===== M0463 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidateFunctions.findNamedFunctions(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 175-204
Documentation:

Method code:
175 |     public void findNamedFunctions(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
176 |       if (!NodeUtil.isStatement(n)) {
177 |         // There aren't any interesting functions here.
178 |         return;
179 |       }
180 | 
181 |       switch (n.getType()) {
182 |         // Functions expressions in the form of:
183 |         //   var fooFn = function(x) { return ... }
184 |         case Token.VAR:
185 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
186 |           Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
187 |           if (nameNode.isName() && nameNode.hasChildren()
188 |               && nameNode.getFirstChild().isFunction()) {
189 |             maybeAddFunction(new FunctionVar(n), t.getModule());
190 |           }
191 |           break;
192 | 
193 |         // Named functions
194 |         // function Foo(x) { return ... }
195 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)
197 |               || parent.isLabel());
198 |           if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
199 |             Function fn = new NamedFunction(n);
200 |             maybeAddFunction(fn, t.getModule());
201 |           }
202 |           break;
203 |       }
204 |     }

===== M0485 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2401-2411
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2401 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2402 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2403 |     int len = s.length();
2404 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2405 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2406 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2407 |         return false;
2408 |       }
2409 |     }
2410 |     return true;
2411 |   }

===== M0505 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.isCandidateFunction(Function)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 335-361
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks if the given function matches the criteria for an inlinable
   * function.
   */
Method code:
335 |   private boolean isCandidateFunction(Function fn) {
336 |     // Don't inline exported functions.
337 |     String fnName = fn.getName();
338 |     if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(fnName)) {
339 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Should we allow internal references to be inlined?
340 |       // An exported name can be replaced externally, any inlined instance
341 |       // would not reflect this change.
342 |       // To allow inlining we need to be able to distinguish between exports
343 |       // that are used in a read-only fashion and those that can be replaced
344 |       // by external definitions.
345 |       return false;
346 |     }
347 | 
348 |     // Don't inline this special function
349 |     if (RenameProperties.RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(fnName)) {
350 |       return false;
351 |     }
352 | 
353 |     // Don't inline if we are specializing and the function can't be fixed up
354 |     if (specializationState != null &&
355 |         !specializationState.canFixupFunction(fn.getFunctionNode())) {
356 |       return false;
357 |     }
358 | 
359 |     Node fnNode = fn.getFunctionNode();
360 |     return injector.doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(fnName, fnNode);
361 |   }

===== M0508 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MarkNoSideEffectCalls.GatherNoSideEffectFunctions.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MarkNoSideEffectCalls.java

119 |     @Override
120 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
121 |       if (!inExterns && hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node)) {
122 |         traversal.report(node, INVALID_NO_SIDE_EFFECT_ANNOTATION);
123 |       }

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
802 |         return item.chain(result);
803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }

===== M0511 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.CallVisitor.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 388-420
Documentation:

Method code:
388 |     @Override
389 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
390 |       switch (n.getType()) {
391 |         // Function calls
392 |         case Token.CALL:
393 |           Node child = n.getFirstChild();
394 |           String name = null;
395 |           // NOTE: The normalization pass insures that local names do not
396 |           // collide with global names.
397 |           if (child.isName()) {
398 |             name = child.getString();
399 |           } else if (child.isFunction()) {
400 |             name = anonFunctionMap.get(child);
401 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(n)) {
402 |             Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isGet(child));
403 |             Node fnIdentifingNode = child.getFirstChild();
404 |             if (fnIdentifingNode.isName()) {
405 |               name = fnIdentifingNode.getString();
406 |             } else if (fnIdentifingNode.isFunction()) {
407 |               name = anonFunctionMap.get(fnIdentifingNode);
408 |             }
409 |           }
410 | 
411 |           if (name != null) {
412 |             FunctionState fs = functionMap.get(name);
413 |             // Only visit call-sites for functions that can be inlined.
414 |             if (fs != null) {

===== M0515 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1905-1907
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1905 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1906 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1907 |   }

===== M0520 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0542 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 295-343
Documentation:

Method code:
295 |     @Override
296 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
297 |       if (n.isName()) {
298 |         String name = n.getString();
299 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
300 |           return;
301 |         }
302 | 
303 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
304 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
305 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
306 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
307 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
308 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
309 |             expectedConst = true;
310 |           } else {
311 |             expectedConst = false;
312 | 
313 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
314 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
315 |             if (var != null) {
316 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
317 |             }
318 | 
319 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
320 |               expectedConst = true;
321 |             } else {
322 |               expectedConst = false;
323 |             }
324 |           }
325 | 
326 |           if (expectedConst) {
327 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
328 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
329 |           } else {
330 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
331 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
332 |           }
333 |         }
334 | 
335 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
336 |         if (value == null) {
337 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
338 |         } else {
339 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
340 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
341 |         }
342 |       }
343 |     }

===== M0549 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 617-634
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
617 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
618 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
619 |         next = c.getNext();
620 |         if (c.isVar()) {
621 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
622 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
623 |           }
624 | 
625 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
626 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
627 |             c.removeChild(name);
628 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
629 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
630 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
631 |           }
632 |         }
633 |       }
634 |     }

===== M0550 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.maybeAddFunction(Function,JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 242-308
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the FunctionState object for the given function. Checks if the
   * given function matches the criteria for an inlinable function.
   */
Method code:
242 |   private void maybeAddFunction(Function fn, JSModule module) {
243 |     String name = fn.getName();
244 |     FunctionState fs = getOrCreateFunctionState(name);
245 | 
246 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Maybe "smarten" FunctionState by adding this logic
247 |     // to it?
248 | 
249 |     // If the function has multiple definitions, don't inline it.
250 |     if (fs.hasExistingFunctionDefinition()) {
251 |       fs.setInline(false);
252 |     } else {
253 |       // verify the function hasn't already been marked as "don't inline"
254 |       if (fs.canInline()) {
255 |         // store it for use when inlining.
256 |         fs.setFn(fn);
257 |         if (injector.isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(
258 |             fn.getFunctionNode())) {
259 |           fs.inlineDirectly(true);
260 |         }
261 | 
262 |         // verify the function meets all the requirements.
263 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Minimum requirement checks are about 5% of the
264 |         // runtime cost of this pass.
265 |         if (!isCandidateFunction(fn)) {
266 |           // It doesn't meet the requirements.
267 |           fs.setInline(false);
268 |         }
269 | 
270 |         // Set the module and gather names that need temporaries.
271 |         if (fs.canInline()) {
272 |           fs.setModule(module);
273 | 
274 |           Node fnNode = fn.getFunctionNode();
275 |           Set<String> namesToAlias =
276 |               FunctionArgumentInjector.findModifiedParameters(fnNode);
277 |           if (!namesToAlias.isEmpty()) {
278 |             fs.inlineDirectly(false);
279 |             fs.setNamesToAlias(namesToAlias);
280 |           }
281 | 
282 |           Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
283 |           if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(block)) {
284 |             fs.setReferencesThis(true);
285 |           }
286 | 
287 |           if (NodeUtil.containsFunction(block)) {
288 |             fs.setHasInnerFunctions(true);
289 |             // If there are inner functions, we can inline into global scope
290 |             // if there are no local vars or named functions.
291 |             // TODO(johnlenz): this can be improved by looking at the possible
292 |             // values for locals.  If there are simple values, or constants
293 |             // we could still inline.
294 |             if (!assumeMinimumCapture && hasLocalNames(fnNode)) {
295 |               fs.setInline(false);
296 |             }
297 |           }
298 |         }
299 | 
300 |         // Check if block inlining is allowed.
301 |         if (fs.canInline() && !fs.canInlineDirectly()) {
302 |           if (!blockFunctionInliningEnabled) {
303 |             fs.setInline(false);
304 |           }
305 |         }
306 |       }
307 |     }
308 |   }

===== M0570 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 374-417
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
374 |   boolean setPrototype(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
375 |     if (prototype == null) {
376 |       return false;
377 |     }
378 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
379 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
380 |       return false;
381 |     }
382 | 
383 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
384 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
385 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
386 | 
387 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
388 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
389 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
390 | 
391 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
392 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
393 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
394 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
395 |     }
396 | 
397 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
398 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
399 |       if (superClass != null) {
400 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       if (isInterface()) {
404 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
405 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
406 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
407 |           }
408 |         }
409 |       }
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
413 |       clearCachedValues();
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     return true;
417 |   }

/**
     * Rewrite named unhoisted functions declarations to a known
     * consistent behavior so we don't to different logic paths for the same

     *    function f() {}

     *    var f = function () {};

===== M0587 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3005-3031
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3005 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3006 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3007 |     if (info == null) {
3008 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3009 |       if (parent == null) {
3010 |         return null;
3011 |       }
3012 | 
3013 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3014 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3015 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3016 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3017 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3018 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3019 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3020 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3021 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3022 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3023 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3024 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3025 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3026 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3027 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3028 |       }
3029 |     }
3030 |     return info;
3031 |   }

/**

235 |     @Override
236 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
237 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
238 |       if (n.isName()) {
239 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
240 |           return;
241 |         }
242 | 
243 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
244 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
245 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
246 |         if (var != null) {
247 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
248 |         }
249 | 
250 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
251 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
252 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
253 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
254 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
255 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
256 |           if (assertOnChange) {
257 |             String name = n.getString();
258 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
259 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
260 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
261 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
262 |           }
263 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
264 |         }
265 |       }
266 |     }

===== M0595 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 341-367
Documentation:

Method code:
341 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
342 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
343 |     // two cases:
344 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
345 |     // and
346 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
347 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
348 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
349 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
350 |     //
351 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
352 |     //
353 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
354 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
355 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
356 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
357 |     //
358 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
359 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
360 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
361 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
362 | 
363 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
364 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
365 |     }
366 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
367 |   }

===== M0598 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 513-541
Documentation:

Method code:
513 |     @Override
514 |     void endFile() {
515 |       super.endFile();
516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

===== M0605 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 112-149
Documentation:

Method code:
112 |   @Override
113 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
114 |     Preconditions.checkState(compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized());
115 | 
116 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, new FindCandidateFunctions());
117 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
118 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
119 |     }
120 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
121 |        new FindCandidatesReferences(fns, anonFns));
122 |     trimCanidatesNotMeetingMinimumRequirements();
123 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
124 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     // Store the set of function names eligible for inlining and use this to
128 |     // prevent function names from being moved into temporaries during
129 |     // expression decomposition. If this movement were allowed it would prevent
130 |     // the Inline callback from finding the function calls.
131 |     //
132 |     // This pass already assumes these are constants, so this is safe for anyone
133 |     // using function inlining.
134 |     //
135 |     Set<String> fnNames = Sets.newHashSet(fns.keySet());
136 |     injector.setKnownConstants(fnNames);
137 | 
138 |     trimCanidatesUsingOnCost();
139 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
140 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
141 |     }
142 |     resolveInlineConflicts();
143 |     decomposeExpressions(fnNames);
144 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
145 |         new CallVisitor(
146 |             fns, anonFns, new Inline(injector, specializationState)));
147 | 
148 |     removeInlinedFunctions();
149 |   }

===== M0619 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.canInlineReferenceDirectly(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 664-722
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a function can be inlined at a particular call site.
   * There are several criteria that the function and reference must hold in
   * order for the functions to be inlined:
   * 1) If a call's arguments have side effects,
   * the corresponding argument in the function must only be referenced once.
   * For instance, this will not be inlined:
   * <pre>
   *     function foo(a) { return a + a }
   *     x = foo(i++);
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
664 |   private CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceDirectly(
665 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode) {
666 |     if (!isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(fnNode)) {
667 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
668 |     }
669 | 
670 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
671 | 
672 |     // CALL NODE: [ NAME, ARG1, ARG2, ... ]
673 |     Node cArg = callNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
674 | 
675 |     // Functions called via 'call' and 'apply' have a this-object as
676 |     // the first parameter, but this is not part of the called function's
677 |     // parameter list.
678 |     if (!callNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
679 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
680 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Support replace this with a value.
681 |         if (cArg == null || !cArg.isThis()) {
682 |           return CanInlineResult.NO;
683 |         }
684 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
685 |       } else {
686 |         // ".apply" call should be filtered before this.
687 |         Preconditions.checkState(!NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectApply(callNode));
688 |       }
689 |     }
690 | 
691 |     // FUNCTION NODE -> LP NODE: [ ARG1, ARG2, ... ]
692 |     Node fnParam = NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode).getFirstChild();
693 |     while (cArg != null || fnParam != null) {
694 |       // For each named parameter check if a mutable argument use more than one.
695 |       if (fnParam != null) {
696 |         if (cArg != null) {
697 |           // Check for arguments that are evaluated more than once.
698 |           // Note: Unlike block inlining, there it is not possible that a
699 |           // parameter reference will be in a loop.
700 |           if (NodeUtil.mayEffectMutableState(cArg)
701 |               && NodeUtil.getNameReferenceCount(
702 |                   block, fnParam.getString()) > 1) {
703 |             return CanInlineResult.NO;
704 |           }
705 |         }
706 | 
707 |         // Move to the next name.
708 |         fnParam = fnParam.getNext();
709 |       }
710 | 
711 |       // For every call argument check for side-effects, even if there
712 |       // isn't a named parameter to match.
713 |       if (cArg != null) {
714 |         if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(cArg)) {
715 |           return CanInlineResult.NO;
716 |         }
717 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
718 |       }
719 |     }
720 | 
721 |     return CanInlineResult.YES;
722 |   }

   */
Method code:
490 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
491 |     switch (n.getType()) {
492 |       case Token.STRING:
493 |       case Token.NUMBER:
494 |       case Token.NULL:
495 |       case Token.TRUE:
496 |       case Token.FALSE:
497 |         return true;
498 |       case Token.NOT:
499 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
500 |       case Token.VOID:
501 |       case Token.NEG:
502 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
503 |       case Token.NAME:
504 |         String name = n.getString();
505 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
506 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
507 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
508 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
509 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
510 |     }
511 | 
512 |     return false;
513 |   }

===== M0631 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.isCandidateUsage(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 427-461
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the name is used in a way that might be a candidate
   *   for inlining.
   */
Method code:
427 |   static boolean isCandidateUsage(Node name) {
428 |     Node parent = name.getParent();
429 |     Preconditions.checkState(name.isName());
430 |     if (parent.isVar() || parent.isFunction()) {
431 |       // This is a declaration.  Duplicate declarations are handle during
432 |       // function candidate gathering.
433 |       return true;
434 |     }
435 | 
436 |     if (parent.isCall() && parent.getFirstChild() == name) {
437 |       // This is a normal reference to the function.
438 |       return true;
439 |     }
440 | 
441 |     // Check for a ".call" to the named function:
442 |     //   CALL
443 |     //     GETPROP/GETELEM
444 |     //       NAME
445 |     //       STRING == "call"
446 |     //     This-Value
447 |     //     Function-parameter-1
448 |     //     ...
449 |     if (NodeUtil.isGet(parent)
450 |          && name == parent.getFirstChild()
451 |          && name.getNext().isString()
452 |          && name.getNext().getString().equals("call")) {
453 |       Node gramps = name.getAncestor(2);
454 |       if (gramps.isCall()
455 |           && gramps.getFirstChild() == parent) {
456 |         // Yep, a ".call".
457 |         return true;
458 |       }
459 |     }
460 |     return false;
461 |   }

===== M0634 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.has(Node,Predicate<Node>,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2659-2677
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2659 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2660 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2661 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2662 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2663 |       return true;
2664 |     }
2665 | 
2666 |     if (!traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2667 |       return false;
2668 |     }
2669 | 
2670 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2671 |       if (has(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred)) {
2672 |         return true;
2673 |       }
2674 |     }
2675 | 
2676 |     return false;
2677 |   }

===== M0644 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addNonEmptyStatement(Node,Context,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 799-855
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a block or expression, substituting a VOID with an empty statement.
   * This is used for "for (...);" and "if (...);" type statements.
   *
   * @param n The node to print.
   * @param context The context to determine how the node should be printed.
   */
Method code:
799 |   private void addNonEmptyStatement(
800 |       Node n, Context context, boolean allowNonBlockChild) {
801 |     Node nodeToProcess = n;
802 | 
803 |     if (!allowNonBlockChild && !n.isBlock()) {
804 |       throw new Error("Missing BLOCK child.");
805 |     }
806 | 
807 |     // Strip unneeded blocks, that is blocks with <2 children unless
808 |     // the CodePrinter specifically wants to keep them.
809 |     if (n.isBlock()) {
810 |       int count = getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2);
811 |       if (count == 0) {
812 |         if (cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks()) {
813 |           cc.beginBlock();
814 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
815 |         } else {
816 |           cc.endStatement(true);
817 |         }
818 |         return;
819 |       }
820 | 
821 |       if (count == 1) {
822 |         // Hack around a couple of browser bugs:
823 |         //   Safari needs a block around function declarations.
824 |         //   IE6/7 needs a block around DOs.
825 |         Node firstAndOnlyChild = getFirstNonEmptyChild(n);
826 |         boolean alwaysWrapInBlock = cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks();
827 |         if (alwaysWrapInBlock || isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(firstAndOnlyChild)) {
828 |           cc.beginBlock();
829 |           add(firstAndOnlyChild, Context.STATEMENT);
830 |           cc.maybeLineBreak();
831 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
832 |           return;
833 |         } else {
834 |           // Continue with the only child.
835 |           nodeToProcess = firstAndOnlyChild;
836 |         }
837 |       }
838 | 
839 |       if (count > 1) {
840 |         context = Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
841 |       }
842 |     }
843 | 
844 |     if (nodeToProcess.isEmpty()) {
845 |       cc.endStatement(true);
846 |     } else {
847 |       add(nodeToProcess, context);
848 | 
849 |       // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions - so any
850 |       // VAR in a statement context needs a call to endStatement() here.
851 |       if (nodeToProcess.isVar()) {
852 |         cc.endStatement();
853 |       }
854 |     }
855 |   }

===== M0671 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
