Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

===== M0678 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M0696 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1236-1359
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1236 |   Node parseInputs() {
1237 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1238 | 
1239 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1240 |     // individual file parse trees.
1241 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1242 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1243 |     }
1244 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1245 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1246 |     }
1247 | 
1248 |     // Parse main js sources.
1249 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1250 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1251 | 
1252 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1253 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1254 | 
1255 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1256 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1257 | 
1258 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1259 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1260 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1261 |     }
1262 | 
1263 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1264 | 
1265 |     try {
1266 |       // Parse externs sources.
1267 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1268 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1269 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1270 |           return null;
1271 |         }
1272 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1273 |       }
1274 | 
1275 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1276 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1277 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1278 |       }
1279 | 
1280 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1281 | 
1282 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1283 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1284 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1285 |           options.closurePass) {
1286 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1287 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1288 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1289 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1290 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1291 |           }
1292 |         }
1293 | 
1294 |         try {
1295 |           inputs =
1296 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1297 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1298 |           staleInputs = true;
1299 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1300 |           report(JSError.make(
1301 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1302 | 
1303 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1304 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1305 |             return null;
1306 |           }
1307 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1308 |           report(JSError.make(
1309 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1310 | 
1311 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1312 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1313 |             return null;
1314 |           }
1315 |         }
1316 |       }
1317 | 
1318 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1319 | 
1320 |       if (staleInputs) {
1321 |         repartitionInputs();
1322 |       }
1323 | 
1324 |       // Build the AST.
1325 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1326 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1327 |         if (n == null) {
1328 |           continue;
1329 |         }
1330 | 
1331 |         if (devMode) {
1332 |           runSanityCheck();
1333 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1334 |             return null;
1335 |           }
1336 |         }
1337 | 
1338 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1339 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1340 | 
1341 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1342 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1343 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1344 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1345 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1346 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1347 |         }
1348 | 
1349 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1350 |       }
1351 | 
1352 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1353 |         return null;
1354 |       }

1356 |     } finally {
1357 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1358 |     }
1359 |   }

   * Gets the {@code prototype} property of this function type. This is
   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.
   */
Method code:
308 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {
309 |     // lazy initialization of the prototype field
310 |     if (prototypeSlot == null) {
311 |       String refName = getReferenceName();
312 |       if (refName == null) {

326 |             null);

328 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }
600 |

602 |     while (it.hasNext()) {

Method code:
1013 |     @Override
1014 |     Node processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression exprNode) {
1015 |       int type = transformTokenType(exprNode.getType());
1016 |       Node operand = transform(exprNode.getOperand());
1017 |       if (type == Token.NEG && operand.isNumber()) {
1018 |         operand.setDouble(-operand.getDouble());
1019 |         return operand;
1020 |       } else {
1021 |         if (type == Token.DELPROP &&
1022 |             !(operand.isGetProp() ||
1023 |               operand.isGetElem() ||
1024 |               operand.isName())) {
1025 |           String msg =
1026 |               "Invalid delete operand. Only properties can be deleted.";
1027 |           errorReporter.error(
1028 |               msg,
1029 |               sourceName,
1030 |               operand.getLineno(), "", 0);
1031 |         } else  if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1032 |           if (!validAssignmentTarget(operand)) {
1033 |             String msg = (type == Token.INC)
1034 |                 ? "invalid increment target"
1035 |                 : "invalid decrement target";
1036 |             errorReporter.error(
1037 |                 msg,
1038 |                 sourceName,
1039 |                 operand.getLineno(), "", 0);
1040 |           }
1041 |         }
1042 | 
1043 |         Node node = newNode(type, operand);
1044 |         if (exprNode.isPostfix()) {
1045 |           node.putBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP, true);
1046 |         }
1047 |         return node;
1048 |       }
1049 |     }

===== M0724 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

161 |       ObjectType thatObj = ObjectType.cast(that);

163 |         return getReferenceName().equals(thatObj.getReferenceName());
164 |       }

166 |     return false;
167 |   }

===== M0739 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0749 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 454-470
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
454 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
455 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
456 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
457 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
458 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
459 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
460 |         String inputName = input.getName();
461 | 
462 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
463 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
464 |         // will get an error down the line.
465 |         inputs.add(input);
466 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
467 |       }
468 |     }
469 |     return inputs;
470 |   }

===== M0750 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1706-1720
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1706 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1707 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1708 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1709 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1710 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1711 |       if (result.last != null) {
1712 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1713 |       }
1714 |       if (result.first == null) {
1715 |         result.first = n2clone;
1716 |       }
1717 |       result.last = n2clone;
1718 |     }
1719 |     return result;
1720 |   }

===== M0752 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.mimimizeCost(FunctionState)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 689-703
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the function is worth inlining and potentially
   * trims references that increase the cost.
   * @return Whether inlining the references lowers the overall cost.
   */
Method code:
689 |   private boolean mimimizeCost(FunctionState fs) {
690 |     if (!inliningLowersCost(fs)) {
691 |       // Try again without Block inlining references
692 |       if (fs.hasBlockInliningReferences()) {
693 |         fs.setRemove(false);
694 |         fs.removeBlockInliningReferences();
695 |         if (!fs.hasReferences() || !inliningLowersCost(fs)) {
696 |           return false;
697 |         }
698 |       } else {
699 |         return false;
700 |       }
701 |     }
702 |     return true;
703 |   }

===== M0764 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 242-305
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
242 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
243 |     this.options = options;
244 |     if (errorManager == null) {
245 |       if (outStream == null) {
246 |         setErrorManager(
247 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
248 |       } else {
249 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
250 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
251 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
252 |         setErrorManager(printer);
253 |       }
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
257 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
258 |       options.checkTypes = true;
259 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
260 |       options.checkTypes = false;
261 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
262 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
263 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
264 |       // parser type warnings.
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
267 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
268 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
269 |     }
270 | 
271 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
272 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
273 |       options.setWarningLevel(
274 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
275 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
276 |     }
277 | 
278 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
279 |       options.setWarningLevel(
280 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
281 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
282 |     }
283 | 
284 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
285 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
286 |     guards.add(
287 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
288 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
289 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
290 | 
291 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
292 | 
293 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
294 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
295 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
296 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
297 |     // error off.
298 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
299 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
300 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
301 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
302 |     }
303 | 
304 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
305 |   }

===== M0765 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1163-1236
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1163 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1164 |     switch (type) {
1165 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1177 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;

===== M0770 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inlineCostDelta(Node,Set<String>,InliningMode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 856-907
Documentation:
/**
   * @return The difference between the function definition cost and
   *     inline cost.
   */
Method code:
856 |   private static int inlineCostDelta(
857 |       Node fnNode, Set<String> namesToAlias, InliningMode mode) {
858 |     // The part of the function that is never inlined:
859 |     //    "function xx(xx,xx){}" (15 + (param count * 3) -1;
860 |     int paramCount = NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode).getChildCount();
861 |     int commaCount = (paramCount > 1) ? paramCount - 1 : 0;
862 |     int costDeltaFunctionOverhead = 15 + commaCount +
863 |         (paramCount * InlineCostEstimator.ESTIMATED_IDENTIFIER_COST);
864 | 
865 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
866 |     if (!block.hasChildren()) {
867 |       // Assume the inline cost is zero for empty functions.
868 |       return -costDeltaFunctionOverhead;
869 |     }
870 | 
871 |     if (mode == InliningMode.DIRECT) {
872 |       // The part of the function that is inlined using direct inlining:
873 |       //    "return " (7)
874 |       return -(costDeltaFunctionOverhead + 7);
875 |     } else {
876 |       int aliasCount = namesToAlias.size();
877 | 
878 |       // Originally, we estimated purely base on the function code size, relying
879 |       // on later optimizations. But that did not produce good results, so here
880 |       // we try to estimate the something closer to the actual inlined coded.
881 | 
882 |       // NOTE 1: Result overhead is only if there is an assignment, but
883 |       // getting that information would require some refactoring.
884 |       // NOTE 2: The aliasing overhead is currently an under-estimate,
885 |       // as some parameters are aliased because of the parameters used.
886 |       // Perhaps we should just assume all parameters will be aliased?
887 |       final int INLINE_BLOCK_OVERHEAD = 4; // "X:{}"
888 |       final int PER_RETURN_OVERHEAD = 2;   // "return" --> "break X"
889 |       final int PER_RETURN_RESULT_OVERHEAD = 3; // "XX="
890 |       final int PER_ALIAS_OVERHEAD = 3; // "XX="
891 | 
892 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Counting the number of returns is relatively expensive
893 |       //   this information should be determined during the traversal and
894 |       //   cached.
895 |       int returnCount = NodeUtil.getNodeTypeReferenceCount(
896 |           block, Token.RETURN, new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
897 |       int resultCount = (returnCount > 0) ? returnCount - 1 : 0;
898 |       int baseOverhead = (returnCount > 0) ? INLINE_BLOCK_OVERHEAD : 0;
899 | 
900 |       int overhead = baseOverhead
901 |           + returnCount * PER_RETURN_OVERHEAD
902 |           + resultCount * PER_RETURN_RESULT_OVERHEAD
903 |           + aliasCount * PER_ALIAS_OVERHEAD;
904 | 
905 |       return (overhead - costDeltaFunctionOverhead);
906 |     }
907 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0776 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-267
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if ((long) x == x && !negativeZero) {
250 |       long value = (long) x;
251 |       long mantissa = value;
252 |       int exp = 0;
253 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
254 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
255 |           mantissa /= 10;
256 |           exp++;
257 |         }
258 |       }
259 |       if (exp > 2) {
260 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
261 |       } else {
262 |         addConstant(Long.toString(value));
263 |       }
264 |     } else {
265 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x));
266 |     }
267 |   }

===== M0778 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.doesLowerCost(Node,int,int,int,int,int,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 797-825
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether inlining will lower cost.
   */
Method code:
797 |   private boolean doesLowerCost(
798 |       Node fnNode, int callCost,
799 |       int directInlines, int costDeltaDirect,
800 |       int blockInlines, int costDeltaBlock,
801 |       boolean removable) {
802 | 
803 |     // Determine the threshold value for this inequality:
804 |     //     inline_cost < call_cost
805 |     // But solve it for the function declaration size so the size of it
806 |     // is only calculated once and terminated early if possible.
807 | 
808 |     int fnInstanceCount = directInlines + blockInlines - (removable ? 1 : 0);
809 |     // Prevent division by zero.
810 |     if (fnInstanceCount == 0) {
811 |       // Special case single reference function that are being block inlined:
812 |       // If the cost of the inline is greater than the function definition size,
813 |       // don't inline.
814 |       if (blockInlines > 0 && costDeltaBlock > 0) {
815 |         return false;
816 |       }
817 |       return true;
818 |     }
819 | 
820 |     int costDelta = (directInlines * costDeltaDirect) +
821 |         (blockInlines * costDeltaBlock);
822 |     int threshold = (callCost - costDelta) / fnInstanceCount;
823 | 
824 |     return InlineCostEstimator.getCost(fnNode, threshold + 1) <= threshold;
825 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.isSupportedCallType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java

/**
   * Only ".call" calls and direct calls to functions are supported.
   * @param callNode The call evaluate.
   * @return Whether the call is of a type that is supported.
   */
Method code:
222 |   private boolean isSupportedCallType(Node callNode) {
223 |     if (!callNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
224 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
225 |         if (!assumeStrictThis) {
226 |           Node thisValue = callNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
227 |           if (thisValue == null || !thisValue.isThis()) {
228 |             return false;
229 |           }
230 |         }
231 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectApply(callNode)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     return true;
237 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
